### 2026-10-08 15:52:27  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Stock Market #Importance 1

FTSE 100 Falls as Oil Prices and Bond-Market Pressure Weigh — London’s FTSE 100 extended its recent decline on Thursday, falling to around 10,400 points, as rising oil prices and renewed pressure in global bond markets weighed on sentiment. Investors also digested Federal Reserve minutes indicating that most policymakers expected another interest-rate increase this year. Brent crude reached a two-week high amid concerns over escalating Middle East tensions and potential energy-supply disruptions, adding to inflation worries. Among individual stocks, Tesco surged more than 3% after first-half adjusted operating profit rose 6.3% to £1.78 billion, prompting the supermarket to raise its full-year guidance and increase its share buyback to £950 million. Imperial Brands gained 3% after announcing a £1.5 billion buyback, while Vodafone fell 1% after raising its annual VodafoneThree cost-saving target to £1 billion by 2032. UK housing sentiment also weakened, with the RICS house-price balance falling to -32% in September from -28% in August.

### 2026-10-08 15:46:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

French Stocks Extend Fall to Over 6-Month Low — The CAC 40 fell 1.1% to 7,687 on Thursday, extending losses from the previous session and hitting its lowest level since March as escalating Middle East tensions weighed on risk sentiment. Reports that the White House had asked the Pentagon to prepare strike options against Iran ahead of the midterm elections lifted oil prices and pushed bond yields higher. Bank of France Governor and ECB Governing Council member Emmanuel Moulin warned that the Middle East crisis is affecting economies globally through its impact on inflation and bond markets. His comments came as investors remained wary on France’s fiscal challenges, including efforts to pass a budget and reduce the country's large deficit. Most stocks traded lower, led by L’Oréal (-1.0%), LVMH (-1.1%), Airbus (-1.5%), and Safran (-2.2%). Financial stocks also remained under pressure, particularly BNP Paribas (-2.3%), Crédit Agricole (-1.6%), and Société Générale (-2.9%).

### 2026-10-08 15:44:18  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Market Regions #Emerging Markets #USDJPY #Impact bearish #EURUSD #S&P500

【Analyst: FX volatility rises, carry-trade risk increases】Analyst Jeremy Boulton says rising market volatility has widened FX swings and left carry trades increasingly fragile; adverse moves can erase interest-differential gains and inflict larger losses. Since Fed September-hike prospects firmed, popular carry currencies have moved sharply: Mexican peso down as much as 9%, South African rand down more than 5%, Hungarian forint down over 7%. Japanese interventions have nudged the yen higher, while a French bond-market rout dented confidence, weighing on the euro and lifting the Swiss franc. The backdrop raises downside risk for carry positions; a further equity pullback could trigger a collapse. With investors taking profits into year-end and uncertainty ahead of the November US election, risk assets may face additional pressure.

Heating Oil Rises Toward Record — US heating oil prices rose toward $4.80 per gallon, moving close to their recent peak of $5.20 reached in mid-September, driven by persistent supply concerns. The potential for further escalation between the US and Iran and continued attacks in the Middle East raised concerns over energy flows despite increased regional shipments. Meanwhile, disruptions to Russia’s refining operations and extended diesel-export restrictions, along with China’s reported suspension of October fuel exports, added to global diesel supply constraints. Hurricane Isaias in the Gulf of Mexico also threatened to disrupt oil and refining operations, with Gulf Coast refineries accounting for about half of US refining capacity of 18.2 million bpd. Against this backdrop, EIA data showed distillate stockpiles, which include diesel and heating oil, fell by 42 thousand barrels in the week ended October 2, leaving inventories 12% below the five-year average.

Gasoline Futures Rise — US gasoline futures rose above $3.30 per gallon, recovering from the previous session’s losses, driven by persistent supply concerns. The potential for further escalation between the US and Iran, along with continued attacks on tankers in the Middle East, kept supply risks elevated despite improving regional shipments. Additionally, Hurricane Isaias threatened to disrupt refinery operations, with Gulf Coast refineries accounting for about half of US refining capacity of 18.2 million bpd. Russia’s restrictions on diesel exports and refinery disruptions, alongside China’s reported suspension of some October fuel exports, also tightened fuel markets. Offering some relief, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories unexpectedly rose by 382 thousand barrels in the week ending October 2, rebounding after two consecutive draws. The accelerated release of remaining IEA emergency oil stocks could also ease some supply pressures.

【Rupee nears record low, tests RBI's remaining defenses】Despite using almost all tools — including rate hikes, measures to attract overseas deposits and tens of billions of dollars of FX intervention — the rupee is closing in on its record low. It sits only about 0.2% from the May trough of 96.97 per dollar, highlighting pressure from a global surge in yields and concerns over high oil prices. RBI intervention has drained FX reserves by $51 billion in the four weeks to Oct. 2, and the first rate increase in nearly four years has not stopped the currency's slide. Ritesh Bhansali, deputy CEO at Mecklai Financial Services Pvt., said the sharp reserve fall is worrying and that intervention is currently the only support for the rupee.

### 2026-10-08 15:24:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Czech Retail Sales Growth Slows in August — Retail sales in the Czech Republic rose by 4.3% year-on-year in August 2026, slightly above market expectations for a 4.2% gain but softer than the previous month’s 5.1% increase. The slowdown was largely driven by lower sales in automotive fuel (-3.2% vs 5.8% in July). However, turnover growth was still broad-based, led by non-food goods, which climbed 7.4% (vs 6.5%), while food sales stood at 3.0%. Among non-food categories, clothing and footwear sales rose 10.0% (vs 8.4%), contributing the most to overall retail growth, while online and mail-order sales jumped 15.0% (vs 12.4%), marking the strongest increase among retailers. Revenues in non-specialized stores with a predominance of food also increased 3.1% (vs 2.9%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales excluding motor vehicles rose 0.2% in August, easing from a slightly upwardly revised 0.7% gain in the preceding period.

Indonesia Motorbike Sales Growth Hits 4-Month High — Motorbike sales in Indonesia surged 15.37% year-on-year to 654,343 units in September 2026, with the number of units sold reaching the highest level since October 2014. It was the strongest growth in motorbike sales since May, accelerating sharply from a 3.18% rise in the previous month. For the first nine months of the year, motorbike sales were 3.71% higher than in the same period last year, reaching 5.02 million units. The Association of Indonesian Motorcycle Industry (AISI) expects sales to remain relatively stable at around 6.4 million to 6.7 million units this year. Last year, motorbike sales grew 1.3% to 6.4 million units, within the target range of 6.4 million to 6.7 million units set by AISI. However, the growth rate was slightly slower than the 1.5% increase recorded in 2024. On a monthly basis, motorbike sales rose 9.7% in September, rebounding from a 6.2% fall in August.

### 2026-10-08 15:17:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Austria Trade Balance Swings to Surplus — Austria recorded a trade surplus of EUR 268.0 million in July 2026, shifting from a deficit of EUR 627.0 million in the same month a year earlier. This marked the largest surplus since February, as export growth outpaced imports. Exports jumped 6.7% year-on-year to EUR 17.6 million, driven largely by increased shipments to both intra-EU and extra-EU countries, which rose 4.8% and 10.8%, respectively. Export growth was supported by machinery and vehicles (+8.2%) and chemical products (+15.9%), offsetting a decline in other goods (-35.1%). Meanwhile, imports grew 1.9% to EUR 17.3 million, lifted by higher purchases from extra-EU countries (+8.9%), while intra-EU countries (-1.4%) recorded declines. Import growth was strongest in machinery and vehicles (+2.4%) and chemical products (+4.4%). In the January–July period, the trade balance recorded a deficit of EUR 3.9 million, as both imports and exports rose at the same pace of 5.7%.

UK Natural Gas Prices Rise to Over 3-Week High — UK natural gas prices climbed above 198 pence per therm, their highest level in more than three weeks, as continued tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz heightened supply concerns. Iran has sharply increased attacks on commercial and energy vessels transiting the waterway, underscoring persistent shipping risks and threatening to disrupt the broader recovery in fuel deliveries. Adding to the upward pressure, forecasts point to cooler weather across Northwestern Europe, which could boost heating demand and increase gas consumption. This weather-driven support comes despite steady additions to European gas storage reserves, with facilities now around 73% full, although inventories remain below levels seen at the same point last year. Meanwhile, the surge in Britain’s wind generation in September has reduced its reliance on gas-fired power generation, but this was not enough to insulate the country from the broader price pressures stemming from ongoing disruptions to Gulf supply.

### 2026-10-08 15:09:43  #MKT News

【TrendForce: Cost pressure shifts to demand; 2027 laptop market may face dual hit to shipments and prices】TrendForce said rising component costs are shifting the 2027 laptop-market risk from supply limits to demand weakness. It currently forecasts global laptop shipments will fall by low single digits in 2027; but if DRAM and CPU prices remain elevated and brands pass costs to consumers, higher retail prices could extend replacement cycles and push the annual shipment decline toward high single digits. For a mainstream $900 MSRP model, CPU, DRAM and SSD together made up about 68% of the laptop BOM in 3Q26, highlighting the growing pricing influence of core components. Brands face a triage: raise retail prices and risk demand erosion, absorb costs and compress gross margins, or cut storage/memory specs and weaken competitiveness. TrendForce expects brands will struggle to simultaneously preserve price, specs and margin, making the degree of cost pass-through a key variable for 2027 demand.

### 2026-10-08 15:07:18  #市場

【機構：成本壓力轉向需求端，2027年筆記本市場或面臨出貨、價格雙重影響】根據TrendForce集邦諮詢最新筆記本產業研究，2027年筆記本市場的主要風險將由供應限制轉向成本上升對需求的影響。TrendForce集邦諮詢目前預期2027年全球筆記本出貨量將呈低個位數衰退，但若DRAM與CPU價格維持高位，品牌被迫擴大成本轉嫁，導致終端售價上漲，可能進一步延長消費者換機週期，全年出貨衰退幅度不排除加劇至高個位數。2026年由於CPU供給改善、品牌提前採購及部分換機需求提前釋放，支撐筆記本市場出貨，但不代表終端需求實質復甦。隨著部分原先預期於下半年及後續釋出的需求提前反映，後續市場換機動能將受到壓縮，加上低成本庫存逐步消化，品牌將更直接面對CPU、DRAM與SSD價格上升的壓力。

【Commerzbank: Reserve Bank of India hikes 25bp; near-term rate cut off the table】Commerzbank analyst Charlie Lay says the Reserve Bank of India has restarted a tightening cycle, hiking the repo rate 25bp to 5.50% and shifting to an “orderly tightening” stance. The move is precautionary to restrain rising inflation expectations while the economy remains resilient. Lay expects the hike to provide near-term support to the rupee, but notes oil prices, US Treasury yields, the dollar and portfolio flows will remain the dominant drivers of INR direction. Commerzbank says short-term rate cuts are no longer a policy option; India’s CPI forecast for FY2026/27 was raised from 5.0% to 5.2%, and Q4 2026 inflation is expected to reach 6.0%, at the top of the RBI’s 2%–6% target band.

【Barclays: High U.S. Treasury yields do not imply cheap valuations】Barclays strategists Demi Hu and Anshul Pradhan say high U.S. Treasury yields do not necessarily mean Treasuries are cheap. If higher yields mainly reflect market expectations for short-term rates, investors earn less extra compensation for holding longer-duration instruments versus rolling into shorter maturities. They note a higher term premium raises compensation but also reflects greater uncertainty and duration risk. A reassessment of near-term monetary policy would mainly affect the front and mid-curve; a sustained rise in the term premium or in the long-run neutral rate would place greater pressure on the long end.

【菲律賓參議院透過法案 擬限制未成年人使用社交媒體】總檯記者獲悉，10月8日，菲律賓參議院三讀透過了一項旨在限制18歲以下未成年人使用社交媒體的法案。據悉，該法案名為《兒童網路安全與保護法案》。法案一旦生效，菲律賓18歲以下的未成年人將不得在設有年齡限制的社交媒體平臺上註冊、訪問、使用、維護、運營或建立賬戶。此外，法案規定，在菲律賓運營的國際社交媒體平臺必須遵守該法案，並需要在菲律賓指定一名代表，與菲律賓方面合作落實該法案。平臺如果違反該法案，將可能面臨500萬至2000萬比索的行政罰款。父母和監護人如果違反法案，也將面臨五千至兩萬比索的罰款，或被要求履行社群服務。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-08 14:37:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Finnish Trade Gap Largest in 4 Years — Finland posted a trade deficit of EUR 1.15 billion in August 2026, widening from EUR 0.81 billion in the same month of the previous year, preliminary estimates showed. This marked the largest trade gap since August 2022, as exports rose less than imports. Exports grew 21.8% year-on-year to EUR 6.33 billion, as sales surged to non-EU countries (+49.3%) and EU markets (+4.5%). Sales to the US (+224.3%), Thailand (+74.9%), Switzerland (+66.0%), Australia (+33.7%), and Turkey (+29.4%) saw the largest increases. Meanwhile, imports rose faster, increasing 24.5% to EUR 7.47 billion. It should be noted that imports from EU countries are now compiled using a new methodology starting January 2026, so only total values are comparable with those of 2025. In the first eight months of 2026, the country’s trade shortfall expanded sharply to EUR 2.80 billion from EUR 0.19 billion in the same period a year ago.

Iron Ore Hits 2-Year Low — Iron ore futures dropped below CNY 700 per ton, reaching their lowest level since September 2024 as Chinese markets reopened after a week-long holiday, with weakening steel margins putting further pressure on prices. Industry data showed that just 6.9% of Chinese steelmakers were profitable by the end of September, while average daily hot metal output, a key indicator of iron ore demand, fell to a six-month low of 2.34 million tons. Reports also indicated that some Chinese steelmakers had begun or were planning equipment maintenance amid mounting losses, hurting the outlook for demand for raw materials such as iron ore. In other news, Australian iron ore miner Fortescue reported lower sales as a prolonged standoff with state-backed buyer China Mineral Resources Group over long-term supply agreements and pricing negotiations continued to weigh on activity.

【以色列重申以色列剋制時代已結束】宣告表示，英國工黨政府自2024年7月上臺以來，採取了多項針對以色列的單方面舉措。英方近期針對約旦河西岸猶太人定居點實施制裁，擴大了暫停對以國防出口許可的範圍，對以色列部分部長實施制裁、單方面承認巴勒斯坦國、恢復對聯合國近東救濟工程處的資助，並凍結英以自由貿易協定談判和軍事合作。 作為反制，以方此前已驅逐在加沙國際救援中心工作的英國代表，叫停了英方在約旦河西岸培訓巴勒斯坦安全部隊的活動，禁止12名涉嫌參與“反以活動”並否定以色列生存權的英國民意代表及公民入境；正式終止英國駐耶路撒冷領事館的外交職能並摘除其徽標。 不過宣告宣稱，雖然英國駐耶路撒冷領事館於當天正式關閉，但根據雙方7日達成的諒解協議，以方出於大局考慮，並考慮到該領事館建築屬於英國政府財產，允許英方在原領事館建築內保留一個由7名外交官組成的工作組，在受到嚴格限制的低限度範圍內維持運轉。 薩爾在宣告中強調，英以在安全和情報領域的合作對保護兩國利益具有極其重要的價值。宣告透露，以色列近期曾向英國傳遞了涉及英國皇家空軍費爾福德基地案件的關鍵情報。以方表示，不希望兩國關係進一步升級，但重申以色列的剋制時代已經結束，任何針對以色列採取敵對行動的國家“都將為此付出代價”。（CCTV國際時訊）

【法國債券遭拋售之後，大型投資者在歐元區債市逢低吸籌】本月法國國債拋售潮蔓延至整個歐元區市場，吸引大型投資者在遭到重挫的資產中逢低佈局，包括義大利國債與企業債。投資者押注，市場對風險傳染的擔憂有些過度。法國國債的這波拋售，推動該國10年期基準國債收益率本月觸及近25年高點，逼近5%。市場愈發擔憂其3.5萬億歐元債務規模的可持續性。衡量投資者恐慌情緒的關鍵指標——法國10年期國債相對德國10年期國債（德債）的利差，已經擴大至1.4個百分點，較月初上漲約三分之二。市場擔憂法國債務危機可能帶來連鎖衝擊，歐元區其他國家國債利差同步走闊，義大利國債利差升至1.1個百分點以上。多家大型資管機構表示，他們已經入場抄底此次拋售中被連帶下跌的各類債券，其判斷是歐元區不會重演十多年前歐元區債務危機那樣的經濟崩盤。安本投資基金經理亞歷克斯・埃弗裡特稱：“這不是21世紀10年代初危機的重演。儘管近期波動劇烈，如今歐洲國債市場擁有堅實得多的制度支撐與市場信心。”他表示，已經新建頭寸押注義大利國債表現將跑贏德國國債。

ASX 200 Extends Slide Amid U.S. Strike Concerns, Price Pressures — Australia's ASX 200 fell 67 points, or 0.8%, to finish at 8,661 on Thursday, marking a second straight session of decline and nearing a one-week low. Sentiment weakened after U.S. stock futures slipped as President Donald Trump ruled out a deal with Iran, with reports suggesting the U.S. military is preparing possible strikes ahead of the midterms. Domestically, October inflation expectations climbed to a four-month high, underscoring persistent price pressures from elevated global energy costs even as the Reserve Bank continues its policy tightening. Losses were broad, led by tech, non-energy minerals, industrial services, and logistics. The four major banks each shed around 1.5%, while BHP Group dropped 2.0% and Rio Tinto slid 2.9%. Fortescue dipped 1.4% amid a standoff with China Mineral Resources Group over long-term iron ore contracts. Other notable laggards included Lottery Corp. (-3.8%), Xero (-3.0%), and Endeavor Group (-1.7%).

### 2026-10-08 14:15:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Imports #Importance 1

German Imports Rebound in August — Germany’s imports rose 0.9% month-on-month to EUR 118.1 billion in August 2026, below market expectations of a 2.8% gain but rebounding from a downwardly revised 5.5% decline in the previous month. Imports from third countries increased 2.2% from a month earlier to EUR 58.8 billion, driven mainly by an 11% rise in purchases from China, while imports from the US fell 0.5% and those from the UK declined 3.8%. Meanwhile, imports from EU member states fell 0.4% to EUR 59.3 billion, dragged down by declines in imports from both eurozone countries, which fell 0.3%, and EU countries outside the eurozone, which dropped 0.7%. On a yearly basis, total imports grew 6%, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.2% increase in the previous month. In the first eight months of the year, Germany’s total imports reached EUR 946.7 billion, up 4.4% from the corresponding period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-08 14:12:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Lithuania Inflation Rate Accelerates Further in September — Lithuania’s annual inflation rate rose to 6.7% year-on-year in September 2026 from 6.1% in August, marking the highest reading since July 2023. The acceleration was mainly driven by faster price growth for transport (20.2% vs 15.0% in August) and housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels (15.9% vs 15.8%). Costs also increased for alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (7.3% vs 7.1%), health (5.7% vs 4.9%), and insurance and financial services (7.3% vs 5.9%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for furnishings, household equipment and maintenance (1.7% vs 2.8%), recreation, sport and culture (5.1% vs 6.2%), education (3.8% vs 5.2%), and information and communication (1.8% vs 2.1%). Deflation in food and non-alcoholic beverages narrowed (-1.6% vs -2.0%), while clothing and footwear prices fell 0.7% after rising 0.8% in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.0% in September, rebounding from a 0.1% decrease in the previous month.

German Exports Drop for 2nd Month — Germany’s exports fell 0.8% month-on-month to €137.6 billion in August 2026, following a downwardly revised 0.5% drop in the previous month and against market expectations for a 0.8% increase. This marked the second consecutive monthly decline in exports, driven by lower shipments to EU member states (-0.6% to €77.5 billion), particularly to non-euro area countries (-3.5% to €23.2 billion), while exports to the euro area increased (0.8% to €54.3 billion). Shipments to third countries also dropped (-1.1% to €60.0 billion), primarily due to weaker demand from the US (-6.3%), which offset increases in exports to China (4.7%) and the UK (16.7%). On a yearly basis, Germany’s exports grew 6.2% in August. For the January-August period, the country's exports were 4.4% higher than in the same period a year ago, totaling €1,092.7 billion.

### 2026-10-08 14:09:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

German Trade Surplus Narrows in August — Germany’s trade surplus narrowed to €19.5 billion in August 2026 from a revised two-and-a-half-year high of €21.6 billion in July, but came slightly above expectations of €19 billion, as exports declined while imports increased. Exports fell 0.8% month-on-month to €137.6 billion, with shipments to EU countries down 0.6% and those to third countries falling 1.1%. Within third countries, exports to the US dropped 6.3%, while shipments to China rose 4.7% and those to the UK jumped 16.7%. Meanwhile, imports increased 0.9% to €118.1 billion, with imports from EU countries down 0.4% but those from third countries rising 2.2%. Imports from China surged 11%, while those from the US fell 0.5% and from the UK declined 3.8%. On an annual basis, exports rose 6.2% and imports increased 5.5%. From January to August, Germany’s trade surplus widened to €146.0 billion, compared with €140.4 billion in the corresponding period last year.

### 2026-10-08 14:07:09  #觀點

【高盛預警：柴油價格或將維持高位至2027年】近日，高盛警告稱，受各國煉油產能受限，疊加各國政府與企業補庫帶動需求回暖影響，柴油價格或將在2027年年底前維持高位。高盛亞太自然資源研究聯席主管尼基爾·班達裡（Nikhil Bhandari）公開表示：“我們需要把成品油價格維持在足夠高位，在明年持續形成一定程度的需求抑制。”該行認為，在當前煉油產能持續受限的背景下，全球柴油價格必須保持高位，才能防止回暖的需求超出煉油廠的產能負荷。儘管該行預計，隨著近期霍爾木茲海峽原油運輸有望逐步恢復正常，布倫特原油價格將企穩在每桶80美元附近，但煉油產能疲弱，將持續限制柴油價格。高盛預測，2027年全球柴油與航空煤油裂解價差（即成品油相對原油的溢價）均值將高於每桶40美元，是過去常規水平（約每桶20美元）的兩倍以上。班達裡預計：“如果明年出現需求反彈，全球煉油體系或將達到過去二十年來最高的開工率。”

Rupiah Lingers Near IDR 17,900 as Dollar Stays Strong — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,900 per U.S. dollar on Thursday, remaining in a tight range in recent sessions as the dollar index held near an 18-month high after the FOMC minutes reinforced a hawkish tone among Fed policymakers amid persistent price pressures. Middle East tensions also kept sentiment cautious, with potential disruptions to energy flows raising concerns over oil prices and Indonesia’s import costs. Meanwhile, domestic fundamentals remained in focus. Fresh data showed consumer confidence edged lower from August’s three-month high, reflecting concerns over elevated food prices and higher non-subsidised fuel costs. Headline inflation hit a three-month high in September, while August imports grew much faster than exports, though the trade surplus stayed stronger than expected. Forex reserves held relatively steady in September, near a five-month peak, but continued rupiah support by Bank Indonesia could put pressure on reserves amid uncertain portfolio inflows.

Indonesia Consumer Morale Eases in September — Indonesia’s consumer confidence index declined to 118.1 in September 2026 from August's three-month high of 118.5. The decline was largely due to lower assessments of current economic conditions, which fell by 1.9 points to a 12-month low of 107.5. Meanwhile, perceptions of job availability over the past six months dropped by 3.0 points to 101.1. At the same time, expectations for business activity decreased by 0.6 points to 121.5. By contrast, sentiment toward durable goods purchases increased by 0.6 points to 106.7. Meanwhile, expectations for income over the next six months climbed by 2.0 points to 136.9. On the other hand, expectations regarding job availability also rose by 2.0 points to 127.7.

### 2026-10-08 13:19:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Finnish Industrial Output Growth Slows in August — Industrial production in Finland rose by 0.8% year-on-year in August 2026, slowing from a slightly upwardly revised 4.6% gain in the previous month. This marked the softest growth in industrial activity since May, driven by a sharp 15.8% decline (vs -12.8% in July) in mining production. Output growth also softened in the manufacturing sector (0.3% vs 5.4%) as lower activity in printing and reproduction of recorded media (-13.2% vs -8.6%), chemical industry (-9.0% vs 0.8%), and manufacture of furniture (-17.5% vs -16.6%) outweighed increased output in the food industry (3.6% vs -1.2%) and textile, clothing and leather industry (2.3% vs -2.5%). On the other hand, production increased at a faster pace for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (7.5% vs 3.1%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial activity decreased by 1.3% in August, slipping further from a 1.1% loss in the preceding period.

Japan Services Sentiment Rises to 7-Month High — Japan’s services sector sentiment index rose to 47.0 in September 2026 from 46.4 in the previous month, reaching its highest level since February and exceeding market expectations of 46.8. The reading marked the fifth consecutive month of improvement, supported by stronger sentiment among household-related businesses, particularly in the services sector, despite weaker housing-related indicators. Corporate confidence, however, eased, weighed down by the manufacturing sector, while labor market conditions continued to improve. Meanwhile, the economic outlook index, which gauges business expectations for the months ahead, fell to 47.4 from August’s six-month high of 48.3, marking its first decline in five months. The deterioration reflected growing concerns over the impact of natural disasters and uncertainty surrounding conditions in the Middle East, although the broader economy continued to show signs of recovery.

NZX 50 Rebounds, Closes 0.1% Higher. — The NZX 50 climbed 8 points, or 0.1%, to close at 13,692 on Thursday, halting declines from the previous session and morning trade, mainly buoyed by gains in communication services, consumer staples, and utilities. However, declines in financials, tech, and consumer discretionary capped the gain. A downbeat session on Wall Street overnight due to higher Treasury yields also limited the rise. Caution rose after the FOMC minutes suggested the Fed is likely to raise rates again this year. Rising oil prices also limited the gain, as they raised concerns over inflation and interest rate hikes. Last month, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised its cash rate by 25 bps to 2.75%, delivering a second straight hike to bring inflation back to the 2% midpoint target. Among the top movers were Australian Foundation (1.5%), A2 Milk (1.4%), F&C Investment (1.4%), AFT Pharmaceuticals (1.3%), Contact Energy (1.3%), South Port NZ (1.2%), Henderson Far East Income (1.0%), and Fisher & Paykel (0.9%).

### 2026-10-08 13:03:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Extends Losses to Second Day — India’s BSE Sensex fell about 0.5% to 72,294 on Thursday, extending losses from the last session as investors weighed the Reserve Bank of India’s surprise rate hike and a renewed rise in oil prices. The RBI raised its repo rate by 25 bps to 5.5% on Wednesday, its first hike in nearly four years, adopting a more hawkish stance and raising concerns over growth. Adding to pressure on Indian equities, Brent crude climbed above $100 a barrel, heightening concerns over inflation and the country’s import bill, while the rupee also weakened 0.4% to 96.775 per dollar, near its record low of 96.96. Meanwhile, all sectors declined except information technology stocks, which bucked the broader decline, with the IT index rising about 1.2%, led by TCS (+2.4%) ahead of its September-quarter results, due later on Thursday and set to mark the start of the earnings season for major Indian companies. 【人民銀行上海總部召開自貿離岸債業務推進會】近日，人民銀行上海總部召開自貿離岸債業務推進會。會議強調，自貿離岸債是服務金融高水平開放、人民幣國際化和上海國際金融中心建設的一個重要改革舉措。各金融機構要提高站位，加強統籌協調，合力推動市場建設邁上新臺階。一是持續擴大發行規模，夯實市場發展根基。深挖發行需求，構建“金融機構先行、實體企業跟進、境外主體逐步參與”的梯次發展格局。豐富中長期品種供給，穩定市場供給預期。二是拓寬投資渠道，激發市場活力。用好FTU投資政策，吸引境外專業機構、主權財富基金、國際組織等全球投資者入市；加快二級市場建設，提升市場流動性。三是立足服務實體經濟本源，凸顯業務價值。對接“走出去"企業境外融資需求，服務“一帶一路"共建國家和地區優質企業融資，提升人民幣資產定價影響力，助力上海人民幣資產全球配置中心建設。四是深化市場培育，加強政策宣介。持續面向全球市場開展推介宣講，總結推廣典型案例，發揮示範效應。五是統籌創新與風險管理，守牢風險底線。風險防控要貫穿業務全流程，保障自貿離岸債業務在法治化、規範化軌道上平穩執行。(中國人民銀行上海總部)

European Gas Prices Climb on Hormuz Shipping Risks — European natural gas prices rose to around €80/MWh, the highest level in more than three weeks, as continued tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz fueled concerns over supply disruptions. Iran has sharply increased attacks on commercial and energy vessels transiting the waterway, highlighting persistent shipping risks and threatening to disrupt the broader recovery in fuel deliveries. Reports that Washington has asked the Pentagon to prepare options for potential strikes on Iran ahead of the November midterm elections further heightened fears of an escalation in the conflict. Further supporting prices, forecasts point to cooler weather across Northwestern Europe, which could boost heating demand as the region heads into winter. This weather-driven support comes amid steady additions to European storage reserves, with inventories now around 73% full. Still, storage levels remain below last year's, leaving the region vulnerable to prolonged supply disruptions and sudden demand spikes.

Malaysian Stocks Slip Further, Hit Weakest Since Nov 2025 — Bursa Malaysia’s KLCI fell 0.6% to 1,602 in early Thursday trading, extending prior losses and hitting its lowest since November 2025. Persistent foreign selling, elevated U.S. Treasury yields, and higher oil prices pressured sentiment. Locally, caution was reinforced by the planned expansion of the KLCI from 30 to 50 constituents in phases beginning December, alongside risks of earnings disappointments amid higher-for-longer costs and supply-chain strains. Still, weakness was capped as traders awaited Budget 2027, to be tabled Friday, with hopes for clarity on fiscal priorities, infrastructure spending, and steps to support consumption. Tech, commercial services, manufacturing, and industrials led sector weakness, offset by strength in healthcare, energy minerals, and retail trade. Banks were among top laggards, including Hong Leong Bank (-2.1%) and CIMB (-1.9%). Other notable decliners included Kellington Group (-2.6%), Sime Darby Property (-1.6%), and Telekom Malaysia (-1.0%).

The exchange said existing three-month TONA futures have not met traders’ needs as BOJ policy adjustment has accelerated; the TFX wholesale head said rate volatility and demand for derivatives are rising. ### 2026-10-08 09:49:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Car Registrations #Importance 1

Indonesia Car Sales Surge 28.1% in September — New car sales in Indonesia jumped 28.1% year-on-year to 79,450 units in September 2026, though easing from a 32.4% surge in August and marking the sixth consecutive month of expansion, according to data from the Association of Indonesian Automotive Industries (GAIKINDO). The deceleration in growth came as the sharper increases in July and August were partly boosted by the GAIKINDO Indonesia International Auto Show (GIIAS) 2026, held from July 29 to August 9. For the first nine months of the year, sales advanced 21% from the same period a year earlier to 679,020 units, highlighting resilient consumer demand despite inflationary pressures and higher non-subsidized fuel prices. GAIKINDO previously targeted domestic new car sales to reach 850,000 units this year. That figure represents 5.8% growth from the 2025 total of 803,687 units. Meanwhile, retail new car sales grew 27.4% from a year earlier to 81,243 units in September. On a monthly basis, car sales fell 2.7%.

【多家在港中資券商調整，內地IP僅可賣出、出金】9月以來，多家在港中資券商陸續通知內地客戶，按監管要求調整相關服務。自內地IP登入香港證券賬戶的境記憶體量投資者，將無法進行買入或開倉交易，也無法向賬戶內轉入資金，但賣出、平倉、賬戶查詢及資金轉出不受影響。據不完全統計，國泰君安國際、國信證券香港、申萬宏源證券香港及申萬宏源期貨香港、中信建投國際、中信期貨國際、東吳證券國際及東吳證券國際期貨、國都香港、興證國際、東方金控等均已釋出相關安排。（介面新聞）

### 2026-10-08 09:47:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Holds Near Multi-Week Lows — The Australian dollar held below $0.70, near multi-week lows as the US dollar strengthened following hawkish Federal Reserve meeting minutes. The minutes showed policymakers viewed inflation as the biggest risk to the outlook, with several participants seeing further rate hikes as appropriate and others judging policy as only mildly restrictive. Still, markets remained skeptical of an October Fed hike, pricing just a 19% probability of a quarter-point increase. In Australia, markets are also pricing just a 25% chance of another RBA hike in November, after last week’s increase to 4.60%. The central bank's October 13 minutes will be on focus, followed by September labor data next week, with the unemployment rate closely watched after rising to a five-year high of 4.6% in August. A steady or higher reading could reduce expectations for further tightening, while the September-quarter CPI report later this month will be crucial, with core inflation expected to remain elevated.

### 2026-10-08 09:46:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Edge Down — The benchmark KOSPI edged lower to around 6,780 on Thursday, extending losses for a third straight session as elevated global bond yields and inflation concerns weighed on equities. The US 10-year Treasury yield climbed to 5.35%, its highest level since 2002, while Federal Reserve minutes showed most officials favored another rate increase by year-end, keeping the outlook for US borrowing costs elevated and weighing particularly on technology stocks. At the same time, Brent crude remained around $100 a barrel, adding to inflation risks and limiting the scope for monetary easing. Losses were seen in Samsung Electronics (-0.56%), SK Square (-4.99%), Hyundai Motor (-3.13%), Hanwha Aerospace (-5.76%), and Doosan Enerbility (-4.97%). However, Samsung Electronics' record third-quarter operating profit estimate of KRW 107.4 trillion, up 782.5% year-on-year and above market forecasts, was driven by strong AI-related demand, lifting memory chip prices and earnings.

### 2026-10-08 09:27:37  #宏觀

【中國代表團完成國際電信聯盟無線電通訊部門第六研究組會議參會任務】據工信部官網，2026年9月21日至10月2日，國際電信聯盟無線電通訊部門第六研究組（SG6）及其下設WP6A、WP6B和WP6C工作組會議在瑞士日內瓦召開。本次會議是2027年世界無線電通訊大會研究週期SG6及其下設工作組的第六次會議。SG6及其下設工作組主要研究廣播電視相關事宜，包括向公眾提供的視聽、多媒體和資料業務。本次會議重點圍繞多媒體移動廣播、廣播核心網、內容來源追溯、隱私保護機制、六自由度空間音訊、超高畫質和高動態範圍（HDR）電視等技術內容展開深入討論，審議透過了多篇建議書和報告的制修訂。我國提交的3篇移動廣播與媒體處理方面文稿被會議採納，參與的3項廣播電視測試與視聽應用方面報告的制修訂取得新進展。此外，會議還介紹了我國提交的關於應急廣播技術與人工智慧生成影片評價方面的2篇資訊文稿。來自國務院相關部門，研究機構、高校、企業等8家單位的24名代表，以線上線下相結合的方式參會。

### 2026-10-08 09:12:32  #MKT News #601318.SS #Impact bullish #601628.SS #CSI300

【National Health Commission, China State Planner issue plan to expand medical rehabilitation and nursing, push insurance coverage and payment reform】China’s National Health Commission and China State Planner issued an Implementation Plan for Expanding and Enhancing Medical Rehabilitation and Nursing. The plan mandates improving multi-source payment systems and, through normal procedures, bringing rehabilitation and nursing services with proven efficacy and urgent public need into basic medical insurance coverage. It directs medical insurance payment reform: adopt disease-based (per-case) payments for short-stay inpatients receiving rehabilitation and nursing and pilot per-diem (bed-day) payments for long-stay inpatients. The plan also calls for refining special-case single-negotiation and other supporting mechanisms, and encourages commercial insurers to develop rehabilitation/nursing products coordinated with basic medical insurance, long-term care insurance and work-injury insurance.

【National Health Commission and CHINA STATE PLANNER accelerate deployment of brain–computer interfaces, embodied intelligence and bionic‑drive tech in medical rehabilitation】China’s National Health Commission and CHINA STATE PLANNER issued an implementation plan to expand medical rehabilitation and nursing capacity, prioritizing R&D and application of frontier technologies in neurorehabilitation, motor rehabilitation and wound repair. They encourage industry leaders, universities, research institutes and hospitals to form industry‑university‑research‑use consortia to speed commercialization and back R&D on brain–computer interfaces (BCIs), embodied intelligence, bionic drive, smart rehab devices, implantable/interventional devices and new biomaterials. The plan calls for development of TCM‑integrated intelligent rehab nursing products and for qualifying innovative rehab devices and drugs to be entered into priority review/approval channels according to procedure. Top hospitals are urged to pilot and document BCI and related product use and to scale innovations across hospitals, community and home care settings.

今日12大單品的終端零售總價大幅回落，八款產品價格下跌，中腰部酒品跌幅沉重。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10759元，較昨日大幅下跌47元，失守10800元整百關口，並創下8天以來新低。

Japanese Shares Fall for Second Session — The Nikkei 225 Index declined 1.1% to below 69,300, while the broader Topix Index fell 1.3% to around 4,100 on Thursday, with both benchmarks extending their losses for a second consecutive session following an overnight selloff on Wall Street as bond yields climbed to multi-decade highs. Minutes from the US Federal Reserve’s September meeting also revealed that all 19 policymakers supported the September rate hike, while most believed another increase would be appropriate by year-end. Meanwhile, oil prices advanced amid concerns over a potential escalation between the US and Iran and continued risks to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, adding to inflationary pressures. Notable declines included Advantest (-1.12%), SoftBank Group (-3%) and Mitsubishi UFJ (-1.8%). In contrast, Kioxia Holdings gained 2.9% after Japan Exchange Group Inc. announced that the memory maker’s free-float weight would increase to 50% from 15% in its latest regular review.

### 2026-10-08 08:26:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Inflation Expectations #Importance 2

Australia Inflation Expectations Hit 4-Month High — Australia’s consumer inflation expectations rose to 5.3% in October 2026, up from 4.9% in the prior two months and reaching their highest level since June. The outlook was reinforced by August data showing trimmed mean CPI remained elevated at 3.6% year-on-year, its highest since September 2024 and above the Reserve Bank of Australia’s 2–3% target range. At its October meeting, the RBA unanimously raised the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6%, its fourth hike this year and lifting borrowing costs to their highest level since 2011. Policymakers said inflation remained elevated, with some upside risks identified in August now materialising. They also noted that strong AI-related demand is driving rapid increases in global prices for technology-related goods. Still, the board said monetary policy was well placed to respond to developments, while remaining focused on its mandate of price stability and full employment.

### 2026-10-08 08:25:47  #公司 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【中金：節後投資者信心有望逐步修復，A股或迎“開門紅”】中金公司研報表示，展望節後，A股或迎來“開門紅”。節前A股在多項外部因素影響下表現偏弱，假期期間海外市場漲多跌少，疊加近期國內經濟資料較平穩，節後三季報有望為市場帶來一定的業績支撐，預計10月投資者信心有望修復，節後A股有望迎來“開門紅”。行業配置上，建議關注兩條主線：1. 景氣成長：AI產業鏈的硬體環節業績普遍高增長，但隨著敘事的變化未來或將面臨分化，部分壁壘不高、產能投放進展較快的環節風險較大，而需求確定性高且產能瓶頸難以緩解的領域有望繼續受益，建議關注光通訊、半導體裝置，以及上游電力瓶頸相關行業。AI產業鏈以外，創新藥（尤其是CXO）、電網裝置整體景氣度較高。2. 週期改善：越來越多領域基本面正在從週期底部回升，建議結合產能週期視角關注供需格局改善的領域，如化工、石化和工程機械等行業，有色金屬各領域基本面較好，但需關注美聯儲緊縮風險對金融屬性的影響。

【從搶著買到打折賣，越南樓市又“變天”？】最近，越南地產股再次跌入債市爆雷後的“至暗時刻”。截至9月30日，越南VN30指數週跌2.46%，多隻熱門地產股猛烈下挫，跌至2022年以來的最低位。2022年，堪稱越南房地產行業近十年來“最悲壯”的一年——大批地產商債務違約，資金鍊瞬間斷裂，破產、清盤輪番上演，留給購房者的只剩數千個爛尾樓專案。如今，越南地產股再次跌進2022年以來的低位，是否預示著危機正捲土重來？近日，記者實地走訪河內、胡志明市的樓盤和中介機構，發現越南房地產業確實再次回到了相似的歷史場景：信貸收縮，流動性緊張，從貸款利率到債券收益率都在大漲——這是一場對樓盤去化和房企現金流的嚴峻考驗。但和幾年前不同的是，行業經歷過一輪出清，能繼續留在牌桌的都是體格更健壯的大玩家；國際產業轉移還在繼續，外資加速佈局越南，房地產的買盤在擴大；政府親歷過崩盤的風浪，正試圖用行政手段和貨幣政策控制過快的擴張節奏。交易量、去化週期、二手房價格……種種資料都反映出今年越南樓市正在降溫。（21財經）

Silver Languishes Near 2-Month Low — Silver held below $60 an ounce on Thursday, remaining close to its lowest level since early August as the latest FOMC minutes pointed to the possibility of another US rate hike this year, while elevated oil prices continued to fuel inflation concerns. Oil shipping: expect a renewed upswing in crude tanker spot rates after the National Day holiday; recommend names positioned to benefit and monitor G7 strategic crude/diesel releases, elevated refining cracking spreads, and seasonal plus cross-regional demand that should lift product tanker volumes. ### 2026-10-08 07:59:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Current Account #Importance 2

Japan Current Account Surplus Above Forecasts — Japan’s current account surplus rose to JPY 4,062.0 billion in August 2026 from JPY 3,628.1 billion in the same month a year earlier, surpassing market expectations for a JPY 3,194 billion surplus. Primary income surplus increased to JPY 5,133.2 billion from JPY 4,222.7 billion a year earlier, while the services deficit narrowed to JPY 115.8 billion from JPY 266.7 billion and the secondary income deficit shrank to JPY 267.7 billion from JPY 441.7 billion. Meanwhile, the goods account shifted to a deficit of JPY 687.7 billion from a surplus of JPY 113.8 billion, as exports rose 16.9%, largely supported by strong demand for AI-related electronics, but imports grew faster at 26.7%, driven partly by higher crude oil purchases. In the first half of 2026, Japan's trade surplus widened to JPY 17.43 trillion from JPY 14.23 trillion in the same period a year earlier.

【險資可投港股通ETF 跨境配置再添新通道】保險資金投資港股通ETF的監管口徑進一步明確。記者獲悉，多家險企近日收到《關於明確保險資金投資港股通ETF監管口徑的函》。函件明確，按照監管規定可投資港股通股票的保險機構，可以投資港股通ETF，並參照保險資金投資港股通股票的相關監管規定執行，該監管口徑自9月20日起實施。8月18日，國家金融監督管理總局表態支援內地保險機構透過滬深港通投資香港交易所買賣基金；9月23日，香港證監會行政總裁梁鳳儀公開證實，內地保險資金已正式登陸ETF通。Wind資料顯示，截至9月末，納入港股通範圍的ETF共31只，合計規模3219億元，全部為股票型。金融監管總局資料顯示，截至2026年二季度末，險資運用餘額突破40萬億元，其中投向股票和證券投資基金合計6.39萬億元，較年初增長6907億元。這意味著，在港股通股票之外，符合條件的險資新增一類標準化、透明度較高的港股配置工具，跨境配置通道進一步拓寬。（經濟參考報）

### 2026-10-08 07:18:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Peru #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Peru Unexpectedly Holds Rate at 4.25% — Peru’s Central Reserve Bank unexpectedly kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 4.25% in October 2026, extending its pause for a 13th consecutive meeting, defying expectations of a 25 bps hike. Annual inflation accelerated to 4.5% in September from 4.4% in August, marking the seventh consecutive month above the central bank’s 1%-3% target range due to higher fuel and electricity prices. Meanwhile, inflation excluding food and energy remained at 4.5%. Excluding transportation, underlying inflation stood at 1.8% and has remained below 2% since April 2025. Twelve-month inflation expectations stood at 3.1%, slightly above the upper limit of the target range. The central bank expects inflation to return toward 2% as supply shocks fade. However, the risks of El Niño and Middle East tensions could have persistent effects on inflation. Current-conditions indicators were mixed in September, while expectations indicators remained in optimistic territory, albeit with some moderation.

### 2026-10-08 07:15:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Colombia Inflation Hits Over 2-Year High — The annual inflation rate in Colombia rose to 6.29% in September 2026 from 6.24% in August, falling short of market expectations of 6.32% but marking the highest reading since July 2024. The latest figure pushed further above the central bank’s target of 3%, driven by stronger price growth for furniture and household maintenance (6.75% vs 6.67% in August), transport (5.27% vs 5.25%), food and non-alcoholic beverages (6.74% vs 6.10%), education (7.49% vs 7.45%), and restaurants and hotels (9.44% vs 9.36%). Housing and utilities inflation was unchanged at 5.74%. Meanwhile, price growth eased for information and communication (4.45% vs 5.97%), recreation and culture (4.91% vs 4.98%), miscellaneous goods and services (5.63% vs 6.05%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.05% vs 6.94%), clothing and footwear (3.12% vs 3.26%), and health (7.79% vs 8.30%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.37% in September, slowing from a 0.39% gain in the preceding period.

### 2026-10-08 07:12:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #RICS House Price Balance #Importance 2

UK House Price Balance Weakens to 3-Month Low — The RICS UK Residential Market Survey showed that the house price balance deteriorated to -32% in September 2026 from -28% in August, which was the highest in five months and worse than market expectations of -30%. It was the lowest figure since June and ended a four-month period during which the indicator had been becoming progressively less negative. The net balance for new buyer enquiries fell to -22%, while the sales expectations balance declined to -6%. London reported a weaker price balance than the national figure, while the North price balance continued to rise. The three-month house price expectations balance stood at -24%, signalling expectations of further near-term pressure, but stabilisation over a 12-month horizon. “A renewed rise in interest rate expectations has created a fresh headwind for the housing market, with buyers becoming a little more cautious and sales activity losing some momentum this month,” said RICS Head of Market Research and Analysis Tarrant Parsons.

### 2026-10-08 07:06:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Current Account #Importance 1

South Korea Current Account Surplus Widens — South Korea’s current account surplus widened to USD 46.11 billion in August 2026, up from USD 42.08 billion in July. The goods account surplus increased to USD 46.81 billion in August from USD 40.43 billion in July, with exports surging 82.1% yoy to USD 104.80 billion, while imports climbed 23.8% to USD 57.99 billion. Exports surged, boosted by strong growth in semiconductor exports amid the global AI boom. Additionally, the services account deficit narrowed to USD 1.68 billion from USD 1.97 billion, due to smaller deficits in the use of intellectual property, while the transportation surplus increased. Meanwhile, the primary income account surplus narrowed to USD 1.92 billion from USD 4.35 billion, while the secondary income deficit increased to USD 0.94 billion from USD 0.73 billion, reflecting higher secondary income payments. For the first eight months of 2026, the current account surplus jumped to USD 279.20 billion from USD 69.67 billion in the same period of 2025.

New Zealand Stocks Extend Losses — The NZX 50 dropped 28 points, or 0.2%, to 13,656 in Thursday morning trade, extending declines from the previous session and touching its lowest level since September 16, tracking a fall on Wall Street overnight due to higher Treasury yields. 【High pay for Strait of Hormuz transits as crews accept ~5% attack risk】A captain described operational changes to oil and LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz amid Iranian threats: night-only runs, strict equipment limits (no lights or mobile phones, typically only one radar), navigation by the Oman coast and lighthouses, and AIS cutters switched off at departure. Crews — largely from the Philippines and India — are being paid premiums, with some contracts shortened but paying several times normal wages and some owners offering an extra six months’ pay; decks are being reinforced with sandbags to guard against drones or missiles. Shipping executives say the chance of being attacked while transiting under US military surveillance is about 1 in 20; Iran has compiled a blacklist of tankers involved in the trade.

Under terms still to be negotiated, Wolfspeed would issue warrants allowing the Pentagon to acquire up to 7.5% of the company in tranches. El Salvador Inflation At Highest Level Since 2023 — Annual inflation in El Salvador rose to 3.60% in September 2026 from 3.19% in August, the highest reading since June 2023. Price growth accelerated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (4.44% vs 3.65%), furniture and household goods (2.12% vs 1.90%), communications (9.63% vs 8.23%), education (3.50% vs 3.30%), restaurants and hotels (0.79% vs 0%) and miscellaneous goods and services (2.23% vs 2.14%). Meanwhile, inflation moderated for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.50% vs 2.62%), clothing and footwear (2.50% vs 2.62%), health (0.23% vs 0.54%) and transport (2.89% vs 2.91%). Deflation deepened in housing and utilities (-0.64% vs -0.32%) and recreation and culture (-0.93% vs -0.91%).

Brazil New-Vehicle Nears 12-year High — Brazil’s new-vehicle sales rebounded 2.3% month over month to 281,400 units in September after falling 1.6% in August, marking the highest monthly volume since December 2014. The increase was driven by strong growth in passenger-car and light-commercial-vehicle sales. Passenger-car sales rose 1.6% to 216,600 units, while light-commercial-vehicle sales grew 4.4% to 52,200 units. Electrified vehicles also supported the market, accounting for 20.1% of new light-vehicle registrations in Brazil in 2026. Heavy-vehicle sales also increased, with truck sales up 2.2% to 10,200 units and bus sales jumping 17.1% to 2,400 units. Anfavea revised up its 2026 forecasts for vehicle sales and production, now expecting the domestic market to reach 3.1 million registered vehicles this year.

Brazil Vehicle Production Falls in September — Brazil’s vehicle production fell 1.1% month-over-month to 268,300 units in September 2026, following a 6.8% increase in August. Passenger car production fell 2.8% to 209,000 units, while light commercial vehicle output jumped 8.6% to 46,200 units. Truck production declined 1.3% to 11,600 units, while bus output slumped 23.2% to 1,400 units. Despite the monthly decline, production reached its highest September level since 2014, rising 10.2% year-on-year. In the first nine months of the year, production rose 8.7% from the same period in 2025 to nearly 2.2 million units. However, of the 173,000 additional vehicles produced compared with last year, only 50,000 were manufactured entirely in Brazil, while the remainder were SKD and CKD models.

【美國消費者信貸增長低於預期 受迴圈信貸下降拖累】美國8月消費者信貸增長低於預期，受到迴圈信貸創近兩年來最大降幅的拖累。美聯儲週三公佈的資料顯示，8月消費者信貸餘額增加83億美元，為三個月來最小增幅；7月增幅經修正後為177億美元。彭經濟學家預估中值為增加150億美元。該報告不包括住房按揭貸款。信用卡及其他迴圈信貸餘額減少48億美元，為2024年11月以來最大降幅。購車貸款和學費貸款等非迴圈信貸8月增加131億美元。行業資料顯示，8月汽車銷量升至去年4月以來最快水平。儘管物價高企，美國消費者仍保持強勁支出，為經濟增長提供支撐。不過，薪資增速已經放緩，個人儲蓄率處於四年低點。低收入家庭以及仍有信用卡欠款的消費者面臨的壓力最為明顯。美聯儲消費者信貸報告顯示，截至8月，需要支付利息的信用卡賬戶平均利率為22.36%，為一年來最高水平。商業銀行發放的60個月期新車貸款年利率為7.54%。

Dollar Index at March 2025-Highs — The dollar index traded around 102.3 on Wednesday, its highest level since March 2025, as volatility in oil markets continued to fuel inflation concerns, reinforcing expectations that the Fed may need to keep monetary policy tighter for longer. Meanwhile, minutes from the September FOMC meeting showed that most Fed officials supported another rate hike this year, although they signalled no urgency to act. The odds of a rate hike in October fell to 17%, from around 70% in the days following the September decision, while the probability of a 25bps hike in December edged up to around 70%. The greenback strengthened broadly, gaining the most against the euro, as political and fiscal concerns in Spain and France weighed on the common currency.

### 2026-10-08 02:27:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

US 10-Year Yield Eases From 24-Year High — The yield on the 10-year Treasury note was at 5.28%, 8 bps below the 24-year high touched earlier in the session as oil prices swung lower and softened the alarm over energy inflation. OPEC+ shipped laden tankers through the Persian Gulf to limit the crunch for global refiners. This coincided with a strong auction for 10-year notes, which stopped through by 1.7 bps. Still, long-maturity yields were close to their highest since the start of the millennium as the risk of high inflation was combined with robust growth, widening budget deficits, and soaring levels of corporate debt supply. Minutes from the Federal Reserve's last meeting showed strong consensus within the FOMC that higher rates are necessary and the labor market is at full employment. Policymakers also noted that the Treasury market is functioning well, but the central bank must be alert to frictions due to high yields. Over 80% of the rate futures market is positioned for at least one more rate hike by the end of the year.

Brent Crude Dips on Easing Supply Concerns — Brent crude eased below the $101-per-barrel mark on Wednesday, extending a slide from September's two-month peak on signs of easing Middle Eastern supply risks. Saudi Arabia cut official selling prices for its flagship crude grade to $5 below the regional benchmark as tankers were able to depart the Persian Gulf with full cargoes. Evidence of a pickup in export volumes from regional OPEC+ producers coincided with reports that US and Iranian officials were holding talks with regional authorities this week. Further easing supply concerns, Saudi Arabia said its East-West pipeline was operating at full capacity, diverting up to 5.8 million bpd of crude to the Red Sea. Still, shipping costs remained elevated following reported strikes against vessels in the Strait of Hormuz. Stateside, EIA data showed crude stockpiles fell by over 3 million barrels last week, while the Strategic Petroleum Reserve remained pinned near multi-decade lows.

Fed Likely to Raise Rates Again This Year — Most Fed policymakers judged that another increase in the target range for the federal funds rate would likely be appropriate by year-end. However, they emphasized that they approached each meeting with an open mind and that future decisions would depend on incoming economic data, its implications for the outlook, and the balance of risks, according to the minutes of the September 15–16, 2026 FOMC meeting. Minutes also showed that all policymakers supported raising the target range for the federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75%-4%. Participants generally emphasized that inflation remained elevated while the labor market appeared to be near full employment, with some signs of strengthening, and that economic activity was expanding at a solid pace. Furthermore, almost all participants assessed that, while inflation risks were tilted to the upside, risks to the labor market had diminished and were now broadly balanced.

US Equities Trim Losses — US stock indices eased from record highs as concerns of high bond yields returned to the market's forefront, risking earnings projections on credit-sensitive sectors. 【五名前巴克萊交易員操縱基準利率案定罪被英國上訴法院推翻】五名前巴克萊交易員曾在一系列具有標誌性的欺詐案中被判操縱基準利率罪名成立，如今其定罪已被倫敦一家法院推翻。英國上訴法院法官撤銷了Alex Pabon、Jay Merchant和Jonathan Mathew的定罪。三人2016年因協助操縱倫敦銀行間同業拆借利率（Libor）被判串謀欺詐罪成立併入獄。另外兩名前巴克萊交易員Philippe Moryoussef和Colin Bermingham分別於2018年和2019年被判操縱歐元銀行間同業拆借利率（Euribor）罪名成立。此前，前瑞銀集團交易員Tom Hayes的定罪已被英國最高法院推翻。Hayes是首名因操縱利率被定罪的人士，也是其中最受矚目的人物之一。最高法院法官裁定，Hayes案審判中陪審團受到錯誤指引，他和另一名交易員Carlo Palombo應被宣告無罪。

### 2026-10-08 00:47:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Falls to 1-Year Low — Palladium futures fell below $1,230 an ounce to their lowest level since September 2025, pressured by investment flows and a weaker demand outlook. Elevated energy prices have fueled concerns over persistent inflation and debt affordability, pushing sovereign yields to multi-year highs and increasing the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding assets such as palladium. Additional supply could also weigh on prices, as a state-owned firm in Zimbabwe, the world’s third-largest platinum-group metal reserves, plans to develop a mining project in Darwendale next year. Over the longer term, however, rapid AI infrastructure expansion could support demand. Adding to the bearish pressure in the long-term, UBS forecasts a market surplus for the metal, as a decline in automotive demand is expected to more than offset lower mined output in South Africa, where elevated operating costs and power grid instability remain concerns, and Russia, where ongoing trade risks continue to weigh on supply.

【Hormuz shipping disruption lifts VLCC freight to $77 mln】Shipping disruption in the Strait of Hormuz has sharply raised global tanker supply-chain costs. Crude Oil Eases to 1-Month Low — Crude oil eased below the $89-per-barrel mark on Wednesday, marking its lowest level in over a month and extending a 16% slide from September's four-month peak on signs of easing Middle Eastern supply risks. Saudi Arabia cut official selling prices for its flagship crude grade to $5 below the regional benchmark as tankers were able to depart the Persian Gulf with full cargoes. Evidence of a pickup in export volumes from regional OPEC+ producers coincided with reports that US and Iranian officials were holding talks with regional authorities this week. Further easing supply concerns, Saudi Arabia said its East-West pipeline was operating at full capacity, diverting up to 5.8 million bpd of crude to the Red Sea. Still, shipping costs remained elevated following reported strikes against vessels in the Strait of Hormuz. Stateside, EIA data showed crude stockpiles fell by over 3 million barrels last week, while the Strategic Petroleum Reserve remained pinned near multi-decade lows.

Platinum Falls to Nine-Week Low — Platinum futures fell below $1,700 an ounce to their lowest level in just over two months, pressured by investment flows and a weaker demand outlook. Bearish sentiment was reinforced by the World Platinum Investment Council’s forecast of a market surplus in 2026, with total demand expected to decline 18%, including a 32% drop in Chinese jewelry demand and a 4% decline from automakers. Additional supply could also weigh on prices, as a state-owned firm in Zimbabwe, the world’s third-largest platinum reserves, plans to develop a mining project in Darwendale next year, with an estimated 44 million ounces of platinum-group metals. Over the longer term, however, rapid AI infrastructure expansion could support demand.

France Stocks Drop on Fiscal and Geopolitical Risks — The CAC 40 fell 1.2% to close at 7,769 on Wednesday, reversing the previous session’s gains and hitting its lowest level since March as geopolitical tensions and concerns over France’s fiscal outlook weighed on sentiment. Oil prices rose on renewed supply risks, raising inflationary pressures, while global yields resumed their rally. Meanwhile, ECB Governing Council member Emmanuel Moulin said France’s bond-market situation is serious but does not warrant intervention. Concerns over the government deficit persisted, weighing on credit-sensitive stocks. BNP Paribas shed 3.9% and Societe Generale lost 5%. Industrials also declined, with Safran down 3% and Schneider Electric losing 2.3%. On the economic front, France’s trade deficit narrowed to €6.1 billion in August from a revised €6.6 billion in July, beating expectations for a €6.5 billion shortfall. Luxury stocks outperformed, with LVMH up 0.7%.

【霍爾木茲海峽受阻致油運成本飆升至驚人新高】租用超大型油輪運輸原油的價格飆升至新高，為石油供應鏈增添了鉅額成本。根據倫敦波羅的海交易所週三的資料，租用一艘超大型油輪（VLCC）將美國原油運往亞洲的成本為7700萬美元。2025年的平均水平為920萬美元。 儘管原油期貨主力價格目前在每桶100美元左右交易，但這些期貨通常基於出口地的原油品級。對於200萬桶的貨物而言，運費意味著每桶額外增加約38.50美元的交付成本。在西非等部分市場，出口商被迫在出口地對貨物進行折價銷售。伊朗戰爭給油輪市場帶來了混亂，重塑了石油從中東運出的方式，並大幅增加了運輸時間。儘管該地區原油流量近幾周有所回升，但貿易流程遠比伊朗戰爭前複雜，導致交付時間延長，實際上大幅削減了船隊有效運力。

FTSE MIB Plunges on Wednesday — The FTSE MIB plunged 2.5% to 49,972 on Wednesday, tracking a global selloff in bond markets that weighed across sectors. Sovereign yields came under renewed pressure amid concerns over debt sustainability, particularly in France, while elevated energy prices kept inflation risks in focus as tensions around the Strait of Hormuz persisted. Financial stocks underperformed as higher yields pressured banks, with UniCredit, MPS, Intesa Sanpaolo and BPER Banca falling between 3.6% and 4.2%. Technology stocks also declined sharply, mirroring broader sector losses, with Technoprobe leading the index lower at 6%, followed by Prysmian (-4.7%) and STMicroelectronics (-4.1%). Defense stocks weakened as well, led by Leonardo (-2.6%). Stellantis bucked the trend, rising 1.4% as the EU considers restrictions on Chinese hybrid imports to support European automakers, that have been pressured by surging sales from Chinese automakers.

Central Bank of Kenya Leaves Rates Steady — The central bank of Kenya left its key interest rate steady at 8.75% for a fourth consecutive meeting in October 2026, saying it saw inflation staying within its target range in the short term despite edging up in the past three months. Inflation in Kenya reached 6.8% in September 2026, its highest level since January 2024. Policymakers noted that government interventions, including subsidies and the temporary reduction of VAT on fuel, continue to mitigate inflationary pressures. The central bank also revised upwards its 2026 growth forecast to 5% from 4.9% seen in August, with El Niño representing a key risk to growth.

【日本東證股指成分股將大幅減少】日本東京證券交易所母公司日本交易所集團7日表示，東京證券交易所股票價格指數成分股將於10月底開始調整，調整後成分股數量將減少約40%至986只。此次調整的核心是成分股篩選標準發生變化。此前，原東證一部（現主要市場）上市企業基本都被納入東證股指。新規則下，上市企業能否進入東證股指，將取決於股票交易活躍度和流通股市值等指標，不再主要取決於企業在哪個市場上市。日本交易所集團表示，目前有683只股票不再符合納入條件，隨後在東證股指中的權重將逐步降低，到2028年7月降至零。同時，標準市場和增長市場中符合條件的股票也將被納入東證股指。這兩個市場此次共有35只股票入選，包括日本麥當勞控股公司、東映動畫、打車應用“GO”等。（新華社）

【New York Fed: U.S. one-year inflation expectations rise to 3.9%, highest in over three years】New York Fed September consumer survey: one-year inflation expectations rose to 3.9% from 3.6% in August, the highest since May 2023. Three-year expectations increased to 3.3% from 3.2%; five-year expectations remained at 3.0%. Households raised expected price gains for gasoline, food, rent, medical care and college. Respondents downgraded assessments of current and future finances and reported tighter perceived access to credit, though concern about inability to repay debt eased. Labor-market expectations improved: respondents saw a lower probability of higher unemployment in a year, reported less worry about involuntary job loss, and were more confident about finding a new job if displaced.

US Inflation Expectations Rise to Three-Year High — Median US inflation expectations for the year ahead rose 0.3 percentage point to 3.9% in September 2026, the highest level since May 2023, according to the latest Survey of Consumer Expectations. Year-ahead price expectations increased across several key categories. Gasoline expectations rose 1.7 percentage points to 4.6%, food to 5.3%, and medical care to 9.1%. Expectations for college tuition increased to 6.1%, while rent rose to 6.6%. At the three-year horizon, inflation expectations edged up 0.1 percentage point to 3.3%, while five-year expectations were unchanged at 3.0%.

### 2026-10-07 22:50:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Albania #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Albania Leaves Key Rate Unchanged — The Bank of Albania kpet its benchmark rate unchanged at 2.5% at its October 2026 meeting, as policymakers stated that the current monetary stance remains appropriate to support inflation's return to target. Inflation averaged 3.3% in July-August, driven by higher fuel and unprocessed food prices, as global commodity costs rose amid heightened geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Meanwhile, core and domestic inflation eased, although tight labor market conditions and rapid wage growth continued to pose upside risks over the medium-term. Economic activity remained solid, expanding 3.6% year-on-year in Q2, supported by consumption and investment. The central bank expects inflation to rise moderately over the next two quarters before gradually returning toward target over the medium term. Future policy decisions will depend on incoming inflation data, fiscal policy and exchange-rate developments.

【摩根士丹利再度建議做空歐元兌瑞郎，歐元區風險抬升】摩根士丹利策略師重新推薦做空歐元兌瑞郎，押注避險情緒升溫、歐元區資產風險溢價上行將拖累歐元。David Adams、Koichi Sugisaki、Andrew Watrous、Alexander Scholefield 在週三研報中寫道，做空歐元兌瑞郎可對沖 “財政與政治風險溢價進一步走高”，以及歐央行因債市波動而轉向更鴿派定價的風險。策略師表示：“儘管市場高度關注利差走闊，但歐元風險溢價的漲幅並不算大。”研報提到，瑞士央行對瑞郎走強的擔憂是該歐元兌瑞郎空頭交易的風險因素，有可能推高這一貨幣對。策略師建議做空歐元兌瑞郎，目標位 0.90，止損位 0.96。該行繼續推薦做空歐元兌瑞郎，預期歐元對多數貨幣將普遍走弱。策略師稱，歐元兌瑞郎大機率延續跌勢，但技術面與持倉情況顯示，短期可能進入盤整。

Brent Approaches $102 — Brent prices edged higher on Wednesday, approaching $102 a barrel, as concerns over further supply disruptions in the Middle East intensified despite signs that crude flows are gradually recovering. The Houthis claimed new attacks on airports and military facilities in Saudi Arabia on Wednesday, while a spokesperson for the Saudi-led coalition fighting the Houthis said a ballistic missile had been intercepted north of Riyadh. Meanwhile, reports showed Iran has intensified attacks on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz in recent days. Despite the latest developments, energy flows from the Persian Gulf are showing signs of recovery. Tankers have continued to pass through the Strait of Hormuz, albeit discreetly, despite heightened security risks, while Saudi Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline has restored crude pumping capacity to 5.8 million barrels per day. In the US, EIA data showed that crude inventories fell by 3.186 million barrels last week, the largest weekly draw in five weeks.

Swiss Franc Weakens as Dollar Gains on Fed Outlook — The Swiss franc weakened to 0.83 per USD as investors favored the greenback amid expectations that the Federal Reserve will maintain a tighter policy stance, while the Swiss National Bank is widely expected to keep rates unchanged. Although weaker-than-expected US jobs data reduced expectations of a Fed hike this month, markets still anticipate another increase in December and at least one more in early 2027. Meanwhile, economists expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% over the coming years, despite markets pricing in up to three hikes next year. Swiss inflation rose to 1.0% in September, driven by higher energy costs and reaching the midpoint of the SNB’s target range. However, safe-haven demand for the franc, amid higher oil prices and French fiscal concerns, is expected to limit imported inflation and keep core inflation subdued. The franc also benefits from Switzerland’s current-account and budget surpluses, a credible central bank, and a highly liquid currency market.

### 2026-10-07 22:08:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Interest Rate #Importance 1

National Bank of Poland Holds Rate Steady — The National Bank of Poland left its reference policy rate unchanged at 3.75% at its October 2026 meeting, in line with expectations, marking the sixth consecutive meeting with rates at their lowest level since 2022. The decision came as government measures aimed at reducing fuel prices at the pump gave policymakers additional room to assess the inflation outlook. Still, headline inflation remains elevated, having accelerated to 4% in September due to higher fuel prices for private vehicles and reaching above the upper limit of the central bank's target of 2.5±1%. Policymakers highlighted uncertainty surrounding inflation and global activity, particularly the Middle East conflict, while other risks also include fiscal policy, energy price regulations, domestic activity and wage dynamics. The NBP also signaled it may intervene in the foreign exchange market.

TSX Falls as Banks and Miners Retreat — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell more than 1% below 35,500 on Wednesday amid elevated oil prices and rising bond yields. Meanwhile, gold prices declined, weighing on miners, with Agnico Eagle, Barrick, WPM and Franco-Nevada falling around 3% each. ### 2026-10-07 21:51:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand Weakens as Dollar and Oil Prices Rise — The South African rand weakened to 16.70 per US dollar on Wednesday, its lowest level since late July, pressured by a stronger dollar and higher oil prices that weighed on risk-sensitive assets. The dollar remained supported by expectations of at least one more Fed rate hike this year, followed by further tightening in 2027. Meanwhile, Brent crude held above $100 a barrel amid continued Middle East supply risks and a strong hurricane forecast for US oil-producing regions. In South Africa, the central bank said that the risk of second-round inflation effects had become more pronounced as oil prices remained elevated for longer and El Niño clouded the outlook. The rand has also faced pressure from high petroleum imports, which have sustained dollar demand and increased the currency’s sensitivity to oil prices. South African policymakers raised interest rates by 25 basis points to 7.25% in September, with markets now pricing in two further increases over the next six months.

### 2026-10-07 21:46:29  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【美國參議員質疑貝森特美債回購操作 稱其干預行動“空前且混亂”】美國民主黨參議員伊麗莎白·沃倫就她所稱的美國國債市場“空前且混亂”的干預行動，向美國財政部長斯科特·貝森特提出了一系列問題。沃倫是參議院銀行委員會資深民主黨成員。她在10月7日致貝森特的一封信中詢問，財政部是否計劃縮減現金儲備，以為國債回購提供資金。她還追問財政部還在採取哪些措施，以壓低長期美國國債收益率，並要求貝森特在10月21日前作出答覆。貝森特此前擴大國債回購計劃，令市場參與者感到意外。該決定於8月19日宣佈，距離財政部公佈季度債務管理計劃僅兩週。沃倫表示，政府近期的做法正在削弱財政部長期以來在發債策略上堅持的“定期且可預測”原則。在長期借貸成本升至21世紀初以來最高水平後，貝森特採取了上述行動。這一輪收益率上升很大程度上由伊朗戰爭引發的通脹壓力推動。儘管財政部擴大了回購操作，基準10年期美國國債收益率仍繼續攀升，本週觸及2002年以來最高水平。沃倫還詢問貝森特，財政部是否評估過更高國債收益率對汽車貸款、住房貸款等面向消費者的借貸利率有何影響。她同時追問，財政部是否會動用財政部一般賬戶(TGA)資金，以擴大國債回購規模。

US Stocks Pull Back from Records — US stock indices eased from record highs as higher bond yields rebounded and hampered the earnings projections on debt-sensitive sectors. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 fell 0.5% and 0.7%, respectively, from their records, and the Dow dropped 1%. Iranian strikes on vessels crossing the Strait of Hormuz worsened soaring shipping premiums faced by tankers in the region and raised the outlook for energy inflation. Long-dated borrowing costs also remained supported by widening federal budget deficits. AI infrastructure stocks erased their gains from earlier this week amid the increasing impact of high yields and credit spreads on their orders, with Micron, AMD, Oracle, Lam Research, and Applied Materials dropping 2%. This was despite positive news for the sector, with SpaceX set to borrow $40 billion to buy Nvidia chips. Nvidia was 1% down and SpaceX fell 3%. Meanwhile, pharmaceuticals and consumer staple companies were higher.

【西班牙工會號召舉行大罷工 反對黨稱此舉旨在服務桑切斯政治議程】西班牙最大的幾個工會準備號召於11月11日舉行大罷工，以聚焦住房問題。屆時，定於11月29日舉行的提前大選即將拉開競選序幕。此前，由反對黨主導的議會否決了一項旨在提高住房可負擔性的法案，工會此次行動正是對此作出的回應。該法案遭否決後，社會黨籍首相佩德羅·桑切斯宣佈舉行提前大選。司法部長Félix Bolaños週三接受採訪時表示，“我認同這次罷工的訴求。西班牙社會普遍認為住房市場執行不正常。右翼和極右翼又採取了什麼措施？什麼都沒有。”反對黨則稱，這些抗議活動是動員選民、影響選舉的一項策略。人民黨發言人Jaime de los Santos表示，“過去有太多理由可以為了捍衛勞動者權利而舉行示威，但他們並沒有這麼做。這顯然是一項政治決定，目的是服務於佩德羅·桑切斯的政治議程。”工會將在週三晚些時候透過投票正式確認罷工日期。工會要求政府幹預住房市場以遏制價格上漲，同時要求提高工資，因為薪資漲幅遠遠落後於住房成本的上漲。

【敘利亞有望成為伊拉克原油繞過霍爾木茲海峽的替代出口路線】目前，大批卡車正排隊等候，準備將原油經陸路運至地中海沿岸的港口。敘利亞有望成為伊拉克原油繞過霍爾木茲海峽的替代出口路線。目前，大批卡車正排隊等候，準備將原油經陸路運至地中海沿岸的港口。敘利亞國有石油公司執行長優素福·基布拉維在接受採訪時表示，伊拉克政府已請求敘利亞協助出口原油，這將在現有的燃料油運輸量基礎上進一步增加。基布拉維表示：“我預計透過卡車經敘利亞出口伊拉克原油的工作將盡快啟動，因為兩國政府之間的協議已接近達成。因此，希望到10月中旬，首批滿載原油的卡車車隊就能抵達敘利亞。”目前，伊拉克已在使用一支卡車車隊將其部分燃料油運至敘利亞地中海沿岸的巴尼亞斯港，貨物在此裝上油輪後繼續出口。在霍爾木茲海峽關閉對其財政造成重創後，巴格達正試圖鞏固其石油收入。除敘利亞外，伊拉克還透過約旦運送滿載燃料油的卡車。交易商稱，少量原油——也是伊拉克經濟的支柱——也以同樣的方式透過卡車運輸。

### 2026-10-07 21:11:44  #公司 #市場

【國際貨幣基金組織預計高油價將持續至明年】國際貨幣基金組織（IMF）總裁克里斯塔利娜·格奧爾基耶娃7日在新加坡表示，即使當前海灣地區的戰事很快結束，高能源價格仍可能持續一段時間。格奧爾基耶娃當天在國際貨幣基金組織和世界銀行集團2026年秋季年會預熱活動上致辭時說，從目前布倫特原油期貨價格走勢看，預計高油價將持續至2027年。她說：“到目前為止，能源價格衝擊雖然規模較大，但總體仍在可控範圍內。這主要得益於能源利用效率、燃料來源多元化、儲備釋放、供應鏈靈活性，以及市場正常的供需調節。”她指出，儘管海灣地區能源供應正在緩慢恢復，但受高昂運輸成本等因素影響，國際油價目前仍維持在每桶約100美元的水平。此外，受全球煉油產能不足影響，柴油等成品油零售價格處於歷史高位。格奧爾基耶娃說，海灣地區天然氣供應也仍受到嚴重影響，尤其是液化天然氣運輸渠道受限。只要經由霍爾木茲海峽的航運仍面臨威脅，這種影響就可能持續。“隨著各國補充能源儲備，以及北半球寒冷季節臨近、能源需求上升，能源價格上漲壓力可能進一步加大。”她說。（新華社）

### 2026-10-07 21:11:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Used Car Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Used Car Prices Down for 3rd Month — The Manheim Used Vehicle Value Index, a widely followed gauge of wholesale used-vehicle prices in the US, fell 1.1% mom in September 2026, extending the decline for a third consecutive month. Non-EV prices were down 1.7% while EV prices edged up 0.6%. "We are in the weakest season for wholesale valuations, and as September closed, depreciation was steeper than we typically see this time of year. The first half of the year actually showed more appreciation than usual, even in the face of higher fuel prices. But with the conflict in the Middle East ongoing, diesel prices at record highs, and interest rates climbing rapidly - increasingly worrying both businesses and consumers - wholesale prices have felt the sting" according to Jeremy Robb, Chief Economist, Cox Automotive. Year-on-year, used-vehicle prices fell 0.6%, the first decline since March 2025. Strength among EVs and Compact Cars was more than offset by Midsize Cars, Pickups and SUVs that continue to trend down.

【競購OpenRouter失利，英偉達推進多筆AI賽道大額投資】一名瞭解相關談判的人士稱，英偉達高管向OpenRouter的對接團隊表達了收購意向，並準備開出優厚報價，但在正式出價前，需要更多時間評估這筆交易。而OpenRouter創始人不願等待。最終，英偉達並未發出正式收購要約（此前從未有媒體報道過英偉達曾有意收購這家初創企業），Stripe以80億美元報價拿下OpenRouter。 英偉達的交易團隊迅速轉向其他收購標的。在接下來兩個月，英偉達敲定總價值超1400億美元的各類交易，其中包含一筆1050億美元信貸擔保。截至7月末，英偉達持有近1000億美元股權投資，另有250億美元未來投資承諾，後續還可能達成更多交易。一名知情人士透露，此前未對外披露的訊息顯示，英偉達已討論向人形機器人企業Figure追加10億美元投資。Figure本輪融資前估值約380億美元，該公司正在新一輪募資。（英偉達已是Figure現有投資方，這家企業一年前就以該估值完成超10億美元融資。） 知情人士稱，英偉達還可能收購初創企業，助力自研Nemotron開源大模型，同時佈局其他AI應用賽道。英偉達同意出資60億美元獲取Poolside的軟體授權並吸納其團隊，原因之一就是為推進Nemotron專案。Poolside團隊今年推出Laguna開源權重AI模型。

### 2026-10-07 21:08:34  #市場 #國際

【政治風險升溫歐元承壓 做空押注擴充套件至更多貨幣對 】交易員對歐元前景愈發悲觀，法國面臨的財政挑戰以及歐洲地區新出現的政治風險令投資者感到擔憂。歐元週三兌英鎊跌至16個月低點，兌日元也逼近一年來最低水平。與此同時，一項衡量歐元兌英鎊看漲和看跌需求的指標顯示，交易員兩年來首次轉而青睞英鎊。就在幾天前，歐元兌美元跌至17個月低點。最新走勢表明，交易員已不再只是透過美元來做空歐元。近期歐洲市場遭遇拋售，法國政府面臨倒颱風險，同時財政赤字可能再次大幅超出目標。荷蘭合作銀行策略師Jane Foley表示，“法國未能削減預算赤字所引發的擔憂，又因法國總統大選臨近帶來的不確定性而進一步加劇。”她表示，法國國債收益率上升，以及歐洲高負債國家債券遭遇更廣泛拋售，都在進一步給歐元帶來壓力。隨著歐洲風險上升，交易員正在尋找比歐元兌美元更直接的方式來表達看空歐元的觀點，因為美國財政前景以及美元自身風險溢價方面的擔憂，可能令歐元兌美元的走勢受到干擾。據瞭解相關交易的外匯交易員透露，對沖基金越來越傾向於透過做空歐元兌瑞郎和日元來表達看空歐元的觀點，其次是兌英鎊和美元。

### 2026-10-07 21:06:40  #公司 #國際

【微特稿日本頻發企業客戶資料安全事件】日本多家企業近日接連報告使用者資料安全事件。日本大型旅行社H.I.S.集團7日說，旗下泰國子公司伺服器遭入侵，或導致數百名客戶護照資訊外洩。前一天，西鐵城鐘錶公司等企業也報告伺服器遭入侵，客戶資訊可能洩露。據共同社報道，H.I.S.集團的泰國子公司2025年12月11日收到對伺服器進行未經授權訪問的警報，隨即切斷了相關伺服器與網路的連線並限制訪問。後續調查發現，627名客戶的護照資訊可能外洩，包括姓名、性別、生日、護照號和有效期、過敏資訊。H.I.S.集團就該事件表示道歉，稱先前沒有立即對外公開是因為需要“耗費長時間正確分析和核實”伺服器中儲存的大量資料。網際網路集團GMO旗下子公司GMO研究與人工智慧公司6日說，其調查問卷網站infoQ遭入侵，94.85萬條會員記錄被盜，涉及會員姓名、電郵、住址、電話和密碼。該公司還說，總額290萬日元（約合12.29萬元人民幣）的會員積分獎勵遭擅自兌換為亞馬遜禮品卡程式碼。日本折扣零售企業MrMax公司6日說，企業應用程式和網路商店使用的伺服器客戶資訊外洩，可能影響170萬名客戶，外洩資訊包括姓名、電郵和電話號碼。西鐵城鐘錶公司同日說，由一家簽約公司運營的伺服器遭非法訪問，大約10萬名客戶的個人資訊可能洩露。日本內閣官房長官木原稔在記者會上表示，政府非常重視這一系列事件，將迅速收集資訊以評估損失情況。（新華社）

Arabica Coffee Futures Falls From 1-Month High — Arabica coffee futures fell below $2.95 per pound, after reaching its highest level since early September, as expectations of ample supply from Brazil helped ease concerns over next year's crops. The 2026 harvest from the world's top coffee producer, Brazil, is seen at record numbers, with supply estimated at 67.6 million processed bags, up 19.6% from the previous year. Additionally, exports from Brazil in September jumped to its highest level of shippping volume in 20 months, reinforcing expectations of strong output. Meanwhile, Hedgepoint Global Markets estimates a global surplus of 8.9 million bags for the current crop year. Still, concerns over the El Niño phenomenon continue to underpin prices. With rains returning to most producing regions, growers are entering the fertilizer application period for the 2027/28 crop, although producers currently have reduced purchasing power over the input.

### 2026-10-07 20:35:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Sugar Prices Near 20-Month Highs — US sugar futures were above 20 cents per pound, their highest level since February 2025, as unfavorable weather conditions in major producing countries raised concerns about global production. In Brazil, rains have delayed cane crushing in the Centre-South region and are expected to reduce sugar output this season. A significant portion of the sugarcane crop could be left to be crushed during the next harvest season, potentially extending the current crushing season into late February or early March. The El Niño weather event is also expected to weigh on production. In India, a weak monsoon could curb yields, while scorching summer temperatures in Europe are expected to result in the worst sugar-beet harvest in more than a decade. In Thailand, drought conditions in key sugar-producing regions are also threatening production. Sugar prices have risen more than 8% so far in October and are up around 40% since July.

US 30-Year Mortgage Rates Hit 7.49% — The average 30-year fixed mortgage rate jumped another 19 basis points to 7.49% in the week ended October 2, following an 18-basis-point increase the previous week and reaching its highest level since late November 2023, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. It marked a seventh straight weekly increase, bringing the cumulative rise to 140 basis points since US-Israeli strikes against Iran began in late February. The latest increase followed another rise in Treasury yields, with both 10-year and 30-year rates reaching their highest levels since 2002 amid persistent energy-driven inflation and expectations for at least one more Fed hike this year, followed by further tightening in 2027. Total mortgage applications fell 4.2%, extending their decline to a fifth consecutive week. Refinancing applications dropped 7.5%, while purchase applications fell 2.1%.

### 2026-10-07 18:12:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

North Macedonia Inflation at 3-Month High — The inflation rate in North Macedonia inched up by 2.7% year-on-year in September 2026 from 2.6% in the previous month. This marked the highest reading in three months as costs increased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.7% vs 1.0% in August), clothing and footwear (2.7% vs 2.1%), furniture and household equipment (2.2% vs 1.7%), education (5.4% vs 5.0%), restaurants and hotels (5.8% vs 4.8%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.6% vs 1.9%). Moreover, price growth remained steady for insurance and financial services (at 0.3%). On the other hand, inflation moderated for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.5% vs 9.4%), housing utilities (3.7% vs 3.8%), health (1.3% vs 1.5%), transportation (3.3% vs 3.4%), information and communication (0.4% vs 1.9%), and recreation and culture (6.3% vs 8.8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices stagnated in August, following a 0.4% increase in the preceding period.

### 2026-10-07 17:50:29  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #The Fed (FOMC) #S&P500 #Impact bearish #US10Y #Impact bullish

【BofA: Bonds first time in decades competitive; S&P 10-year returns may trail Treasuries】BofA head of US equities and quant strategy Savita Subramanian said bonds are, for the first time in decades, a genuinely competitive alternative to equities. With the US 10-year yield above 5%, BofA’s valuation model indicates the S&P 500’s annualized return over the next 10 years may be lower than current Treasury yields. She warned high investor sentiment raises equity downside risk and limits further upside. Policymakers, including the Fed and the US Treasury, are monitoring the long end to avoid too-large increases in long-term rates; demographics and potential AI-driven disinflation imply a lower long-term ceiling for US rates than in the 1970s–80s, making sustained Treasury yields above 6–7% unlikely and improving the case for bond allocations.

Mauritius Inflation Rate Reaches Highest in Over 2 Years — The annual inflation rate in Mauritius accelerated sharply to 5.7% in September 2026, the highest level since February 2024, up from 4.9% in August. It marked the third consecutive month of rising inflation, driven mainly by a sharp increase in food price growth, which accelerated to 6.2% from 3.9%, and transport, which rose to 7.6% from 6%. Additional upward pressure came from restaurants and hotels (9% vs 8.6%), furnishings (4.6% vs 4.4%), clothing and footwear (2.5% vs 2.4%), and education (6.3% vs 5.4%). Meanwhile, inflation remained elevated for housing and utilities at 8.9%, while recreation, sport and culture stood at 2.7%. From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.6% following a 0.4% rise in August.

【Cui Dongshu: Chinese domestic-brand new-energy vehicles continue to gain in major overseas markets】CPCA secretary-general Cui Dongshu wrote that in major overseas markets with available data, Chinese domestic-brand new-energy passenger vehicles have continued to strengthen. In 2026 their share of overseas new-energy passenger vehicle sales rose sharply, reaching 29% in August (up 14 percentage points); Jan–Aug 2026 share was 24.8%, up 9 ppts YoY. Cui attributed the gain to stronger exports by domestic NE makers and a severe contraction in the US new-energy vehicle market. Regional penetration stood above 80% in South America and 47% in Southeast Asia.

### 2026-10-07 17:31:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

Sterling Slips as Oil Rebound Revives Inflation Concerns — Sterling traded around $1.32, close to a three-month low against the dollar touched last week, as a rebound in oil prices added to inflation concerns and reinforced expectations for UK interest rates to remain higher for longer. Brent crude rose back above $100 a barrel as Iran intensified attacks on shipping in the Strait of Hormuz, prompting markets to price more than 100 basis points of Bank of England tightening by the end of next year. BoE policymaker Catherine Mann said Tuesday that inflation above the Bank’s 2% target appeared embedded in the economy and warned it could reach 4% around the turn of the year, when wage negotiations typically take place. Meanwhile, the dollar remained supported by expectations that the Federal Reserve will raise rates for a second time this year in December. Against the euro, sterling rose to its highest level since June last year, as concerns over France’s deepening fiscal crisis weighed on the common currency.

### 2026-10-07 17:27:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Luxembourg #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 1

Luxembourg Economy Contracts in Q2 — Luxembourg’s economy contracted 0.9% quarter-on-quarter in the second quarter of 2026, reversing from a revised 0.3% growth in the previous three-month period. This marked the first contraction after six straight quarters of growth, as declines were recorded in gross fixed investment (-1.9% vs 6.3% in Q1), and household expenditure (-0.3% vs -0.8%), while growth eased for government spending (1.1% vs 1.8%). Net trade also weighed on the economy, with both exports (-0.4% vs -1.2%) and imports (-0.8% vs -2.1%) declining at softer rates. On the production side, declines were seen in industry, including energy and water distribution (-3.7% vs -1.6%), construction (-2.1% vs 2.7%), trade, transport, accommodation and food services (-0.8% vs 1.1%), and financial and insurance activities (-1.5% vs -0.7%). Conversely, growth was recorded in agriculture, forestry, and fishing (4.5% vs 0.9%). On an annual basis, GDP grew 1.3% in Q2, slowing from a revised 2.8% rise in the prior quarter.
